    function queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      const type = String(payload.type || "");
      if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      } else if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      }
      pendingFramePayloads.push(payload);
    }

    function pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(payload) {
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return 0;
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") return 1;
      return 2;
    }

        .map((payload, index) => ({ payload, index }))
        .sort((a, b) => pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(a.payload) - pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(b.payload) || a.index - b.index)
        .map(item => item.payload);
      pendingFramePayloads = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index += 1) {
        try {
          postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, queue[index], appFrameSessionId);
        } catch (_) {
          pendingFramePayloads.unshift(...queue.slice(index));
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    }

    function runtimeModelPayloadForReload() {
      if (!productContract) return null;

      return {
        type: "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL",

    function reloadAppFrame(payload = null, opts = {}) {
      const reloadPayload = runtimeModelPayloadForReload();
      if (payload?.type !== "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL" && reloadPayload) queuePendingRuntimePayload(reloadPayload);
      if (payload) queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
      const activeStateId = hostRuntimeStateView();
      if (!payload && opts.preserveCurrent && activeStateId && byId(activeStateId)) {
        pendingAppStartStateId = activeStateId;
        pendingAppStartSuppressLayerFollow = false;
      }

    }

    function postRuntimePayload(payload) {
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      if (appFrameReady && frameEl.contentWindow) {
        if (!appFrameContainsGeneratedApp()) {
          reloadAppFrame(payload);
        } else {
          try {
            postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, payload, appFrameSessionId);
          } catch (_) {
            queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
          }
        }
      } else {
        queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
      }
      return payload;
    }

    function closePresetInfoOverlay() {

        presetInfoPreviewSendTimer = null;

        presetInfoPreviewUrl = "";
      }
    }

      closePresetInfoOverlay();
    }

    function safeExternalAppUrl(url) {
      const raw = String(url || "").trim();
      if (!raw) return "";
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(raw);
        return parsed.protocol === "http:" || parsed.protocol === "https:" ? parsed.href : "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    function simpleContentPresetComponent(template) {
      if (!template?.builtIn) return null;
      const copy = snapshotStateTemplates([template])[0];
      if (!copy) return null;
      const components = normalizeComponents(copy.components);
      if (components.length !== 1 || !componentPresetTypes().includes(components[0].type)) return null;
      if (snapshotTemplateStates(copy).length || snapshotTemplateTransitions(copy).length) return null;
      if (normalizeDataWires(copy.dataWires).length) return null;
      return cloneComponents(components)[0] || null;
    }

      );
      return component?.id || "";
    }

    function validateBoundaryDefinition(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return normalizeBoundaryConfig(null);
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      const allowed = new Set(["entryId", "exitId", "entryDisabled", "exitDisabled", "title", "note"]);
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        if (!allowed.has(key)) throw new Error(`${path}.${key} is not part of the canonical boundary contract.`);
      }
      for (const key of ["entryDisabled", "exitDisabled"]) {
        if (key in value && typeof value[key] !== "boolean") throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must be a boolean.`);
      }
      return {
        entryId: "entryId" in value ? assertString(value.entryId, `${path}.entryId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        exitId: "exitId" in value ? assertString(value.exitId, `${path}.exitId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        entryDisabled: Boolean(value.entryDisabled),
        exitDisabled: Boolean(value.exitDisabled),
        title: "title" in value ? assertString(value.title, `${path}.title`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        note: "note" in value ? assertString(value.note, `${path}.note`, { allowEmpty: true }) : ""
      };
    }

    function validateModelDefinition(source) {
      assertPlainObject(source, "definition.model");
      if (source.version !== DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error("definition.model.version must be 2.");
      assertString(source.name, "definition.model.name");
      const initial = assertString(source.initial, "definition.model.initial", { allowEmpty: true });
      if (!Array.isArray(source.states)) throw new Error("definition.model.states must be an array.");
      if (!Array.isArray(source.transitions)) throw new Error("definition.model.transitions must be an array.");
      if ("editorGroups" in source) throw new Error("definition.model.editorGroups is not part of the contract; use real parent states.");

      const ids = new Set();
      const states = source.states.map((state, index) => {
        const path = `definition.model.states[${index}]`;
        assertPlainObject(state, path);
        if ("renderMode" in state) throw new Error(`${path}.renderMode is not part of the contract; put visible components on the state itself.`);
        const id = assertString(state.id, `${path}.id`);
        if (isReservedRuntimeId(id)) throw new Error(`${path}.id must not use the reserved runtime id namespace.`);
        if (ids.has(id)) throw new Error(`${path}.id must be unique.`);
        ids.add(id);
        const parentId = "parentId" in state ? validateNullableString(state.parentId, `${path}.parentId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "";
        const dataObject = validateStateDataObject(state.data, `${path}.data`);
        return {
          id,
          title: assertString(state.title, `${path}.title`),
          components: validateComponents(state.components, `${path}.components`, { stateId: id }),
          data: dataObject,
          dataTypes: validateDataTypesObject(state.dataTypes, `${path}.dataTypes`, dataObject),
          dataSource: validateDataSource(state.dataSource, `${path}.dataSource`, { fallbackTarget: stateDataScopeForId(id) + ".fetch" }),
          repeat: validateRepeatConfig(state.repeat, `${path}.repeat`),
          dataWires: validateDataWires(state.dataWires, `${path}.dataWires`),
          subscriptions: validateSubscriptions(state.subscriptions, `${path}.subscriptions`),
          boundary: validateBoundaryDefinition(state.boundary, `${path}.boundary`),
          parentId: parentId || null,
          x: assertFiniteNumber(state.x, `${path}.x`),
          y: assertFiniteNumber(state.y, `${path}.y`)
        };
      });

      if (states.length && !initial) throw new Error("definition.model.initial must reference an existing state.");
      if (initial && !ids.has(initial)) throw new Error("definition.model.initial must reference an existing state.");
      const rootBoundary = validateBoundaryDefinition(source.boundary, "definition.model.boundary");
      if (rootBoundary.entryId) {
        const entry = states.find(item => item.id === rootBoundary.entryId);
        if (!entry || entry.parentId) throw new Error("definition.model.boundary.entryId must reference a root state.");
      }
      if (rootBoundary.exitId) {
        const exit = states.find(item => item.id === rootBoundary.exitId);
        if (!exit || exit.par

 function loadEditorState() {

    function applyPersistedEditorState(data) {
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(data.model)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      const nextModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.model)));
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(nextModel)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      model = nextModel;
      currentLayerId = data.currentLayerId && byId(data.currentLayerId) ? data.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(data.selected || null);
      return true;
    }

    function visibleRectHeight(rect) {
      if (!rect || rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return 0;
      const top = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.top));
      const bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.bottom));
      return Math.max(0, bottom - top);
    }

    function mobilePreviewRuntimeBottomInsetPx() {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout() || mobileWorkspaceView !== "app") return 0;
      const commandInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileCommandBarEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const tabsInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileTabsEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const visualViewportBottom = window.visualViewport
        ? window.visualViewport.offsetTop + window.visualViewport.height
        : window.innerHeight;
      const browserInset = Math.max(0, (window.innerHeight || 0) - visualViewportBottom);
      return Math.max(0, Math.round(commandInset + tabsInset + browserInset + 16));
    }

    function applyMobilePreviewRuntimeInset() {
      const inset = mobilePreviewRuntimeBottomInsetPx();
      workspaceEl.style.setProperty("--mobile-runtime-preview-bottom-inset", inset + "px");
      try {
        const doc = frameEl?.contentDocument;
        if (!doc?.documentElement) return;
        const value = inset + "px";
        doc.documentElement.style.setProperty("--zustand-host-bottom-inset", value);
        doc.body?.style.setProperty("--zustand-host-bottom-inset", value);
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    }

    function initHistory() {
      historyCurrent = editorSnapshot();
      undoStack = [];
      redoStack = [];
      beginHistoryAction();
      updateHistoryButtons();

    function restoreRejectedEditorMutation() {
      if (!historyCurrent) throw new Error("A valid editor history snapshot is required before changing the model.");
      const restored = parseEditorSnapshot(historyCurrent);
      const restoredModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(restored.model)));
      const issue = modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(restoredModel);
      if (issue) throw new Error(issue.message);
      model = restoredModel;
      currentLayerId = restored.currentLayerId && byId(restored.currentLayerId) ? restored.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(restored.selected);
      connecting = null;
      connectingTargetId = null;
      dragging = null;
      boxSelecting = null;
      updateSelectRect();
      draw();
      renderStateExplorer();
      refreshInspectorForSelection();
    }

    function saveEditorState(historyKey = null, sync = true) {
      if (!flushingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) {
        restoreRejectedEditorMutation();
        return false;
      }
      selected = normalizeSelection();
      const next = editorSnapshot();
      if (next === historyCurrent) {
        persistModel(sync);
        return true;
      }
      const historyGroup = historyKey ? `${historyActionId}:${historyKey}` : null;
      if (!historyCurrent) {
        historyCurrent = next;
      } else if (historyGroup && historyGroup === lastHistoryGroup && undoStack.length) {
        // Repeated mutations within one explicit user interaction remain one undo step.
      } else {
        undoStack.push(historyCurrent);
        if (undoStack.length > 100) undoStack.shift();
      }
      redoStack = [];
      historyCurrent = next;
      lastHistoryGroup = historyGroup;
      persistModel(sync);
      updateHistoryButtons();
      return true;
    }

    function saveModel(historyKey = null) {
      return saveEditorState(historyKey, true);
    }

    function saveSelection(historyKey = null) {
      return saveEditorState(historyKey, false);
    }

      updateHistoryButtons();
    }

    function undoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!undoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();
      const previous = undoStack.pop();
      redoStack.push(current);
      beginHistoryAction();
      restoreHistorySnapshot(previous);
    }

    function isPlainObject(value) {
      return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
    }

    function assertPlainObject(value, path) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an object.`);
    }

    function assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error(`${path} must be a string.`);
      if (!allowEmpty && !value.trim()) throw new Error(`${path} must not be empty.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertFiniteNumber(value, path) {
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      return value;

    function exportStateComponentFile(s) {
      const snapshot = stateExportSnapshot(s);
      const payload = componentExportPayload("state", { state: snapshot });
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      downloadTextFile(
        JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) + "\n",
        `${normalizeId(snapshot.title || snapshot.id)}-${stamp}.state-component.json`,
        "application/json"
      );
    }

    function saveDefinitionFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      const definition = definitionPayload();
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      const text = JSON.stringify(definition, null, 2) + "\n";
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.state.json`, "application/json");
    }

    async function exportHtmlFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      let text;
      try {
        const payload = await inlineExportImages(definitionPayload());
        const appHtml = typeof GENERATED_APP_HTML === "string" ? GENERATED_APP_HTML : await (await fetch(APP_URL, { cache: "no-store" })).text();
        text = buildStandaloneAppHtml(appHtml, payload);
      } catch (err) {
        await modalAlert(err?.message || "Die eigenständige App konnte nicht exportiert werden.", "Export fehlgeschlagen");
        return;
      }
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.html`, "text/html");
    }

        return;

        const positionChanged = nextX !== s.x || nextY !== s.y;

    function deleteSelectedItems() {
      if (!selected) return false;
      const ids = selectedRealStateIdList();
      const edgeIds = [...new Set([...(selected.edges || []), ...selectedEdgeIdList()])];
      const removable = ids.filter(id => byId(id));
      const removeEdges = new Set(edgeIds);
      if (removable.length) stopRuntimeLayerFollow();
      if (selected.allCurrentLayer && removable.length) {
        const removedNodes = removeStatesById(removable);
        if (!removedNodes) return false;
        selected = null;
        showEmptyInspector();
        saveModel();
        draw();
        return true;
      }
      if (removeEdges.size) {
        const boundaryIds = [];
        for (const edgeId of removeEdges) {
          if (deleteBoundaryFlowById(edgeId)) boundaryIds.push(edgeId);
        }
        model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(t => !removeEdges.has(t.id) && !boundaryIds.includes(t.id));
        const remainingNodes = removable.filter(id => byId(id));
        selected = remainingNodes.length ? selectionFromParts(remainingNodes, []) : null;
        if (!selected) showEmptyInspector();
        saveModel();
        draw();
        return true;
      }
      const removedNodes = removable.length ? removeStatesById(removable) : false;
      if (!removedNodes) return false;
      selected = null;
      showEmptyInspector();
      saveModel();
      draw();
      return true;
    }

    function deleteBoundaryFlowById(edgeId) {
      const transition = model.transitions.find(t => t.id === edgeId);
      const flow = boundaryFlowInfoFromTransition(transition, edgeId);
      if (!flow) return false;
      const parent = boundaryParentFromFlow(flow);
      const side = flow.side;
      if (side === "input" || side === "output") clearBoundaryEndpoint(parent, side);
      else clearBoundaryRoute(parent);
      model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(item => {
        if (item.id === edgeId) return false;
        const itemFlow = boundaryFlowInfoFromTransition(item, item.id);
        return !(itemFlow && itemFlow.parentId === flow.parentId && itemFlow.side === side);
      });
      return true;
    }

    function deleteActiveSelection() {
      if (selected && deleteSelectedItems()) return true;
      if (inspectorEdgeId && deleteBoundaryFlowById(inspectorEdgeId)) {
        selected = null;
        showEmptyInspector();
        saveModel();
        draw();
        return true;
      }
      if (!inspectorEdgeId || !edgeById(inspectorEdgeId)) return false;
      selected = selectionFromParts([], [inspectorEdgeId]);
      return deleteSelectedItems();
    }

    function collapseSelectedStatesToParent() {
      const selectedIds = selectedStateIdsForCollapse();
      if (!selectedIds.length) return false;
      const selectedSet = new Set(selectedIds);
      const states = selectedIds.map(id => byModelId(model, id)).filter(Boolean);
      if (!states.length) return false;
      const entry = collapseEntryStateFor(states, selectedSet);
      const exit = collapseExitStateFor(states, selectedSet);
      if (!entry || !exit) return false;
      const outerParentId = currentLayerId || null;
      const outerParent = currentLayerContainer();
      const outerBoundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(outerParent.rootLayer ? model.boundary : outerParent.boundary);
      const bounds = stateGroupBounds(states);
      const group = makeState(
        snapToGrid((bounds.minX + bounds.maxX) / 2 - NODE_W / 2),
        snapToGrid((bounds.minY + bounds.maxY) / 2 - NODE_H / 2),
        "Group",
        outerParentId
      );
      group.boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig({ entryId: entry.id, exitId: exit.id });
      model.states.push(group);

      for (const transition of model.transitions) {
        if (isBoundaryFlowTransition(transition)) continue;
        const originalFrom = transition.from;
        const originalTo = transition.to;
        const fromSelected = selectedSet.has(originalFrom);
        const toSelected = selectedSet.has(originalTo);
        if (!fromSelected && toSelected) {
          transition.to = group.id;
          transition.groupEntryId = originalTo;
        }
        if (fromSelected && !toSelected) {
          transition.from = group.id;
          transition.groupExitId = originalFrom;
        }
      }

      for (const state of states) {
        state.parentId = group.id;
      }
      replaceNestedBoundaryReferences(selectedSet, group.id, outerParentId);
      if (outerBoundary.entryId && selectedSet.has(outerBoundary.entryId)) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "input", group.id);
      if (outerBoundary.exitId && selectedSet.has(outerBoundary.exitId)) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "output", group.id);
      if (selectedSet.has(model.initial)) model.initial = group.id;
      ensureDefaultBoundaryTransitions(outerParent, statesInLayer(outerParentId));
      ensureDefaultBoundaryTransitions(group, statesInLayer(group.id));
      normalizeModel(model);
      selected = selectionFromParts([group.id], []);
      manualDegroupTargetId = group.id;
      showNodeInspector(group, { focus: false });
      saveModel("group:collapse");
      draw();
      return true;
    }

    function childBoundaryState(parent, side) {
      const children = statesInLayer(parent.id);
      const boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(parent.boundary);
      const configuredId = side === "input" ? boundary.entryId : boundary.exitId;
      const configured = children.find(state => state.id === configuredId);
      return configured || null;
    }

        !String(transition.from || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix) &&
        !String(transition.to || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix)
      );
    }

    function conditionInputSummary(condition) {
      const inputs = inferConditionInputs(condition);
      if (!inputs.length) return "Keine Eingaben. Immer verfügbar.";
      return "Eingaben: " + inputs.map(input => `${input.name}:${input.type}`).join(", ");
    }

    function inferConditionInputs(condition) {
      const map = new Map();
      const upsert = (name, type = "boolean") => {
        const key = String(name || "");
        if (!key || ["true", "false", "and", "or", "null", "undefined", "always"].includes(key.toLowerCase())) return;
        const previous = map.get(key);
        const rank = { boolean: 0, int: 1, float: 2, text: 3 };
        const nextType = rank[type] > rank[previous?.type || "boolean"] ? type : previous?.type || type;
        map.set(key, { name: key, type: nextType });
      };
      const source = String(condition || "");
      const searchable = source.replace(/(["'])(?:\\.|(?!\1)[\s\S])*?\1/g, "");
      for (const name of searchable.match(/[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*/g) || []) upsert(name);
      for (const numeric of searchable.matchAll(/([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*)\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g)) {
        upsert(numeric[1], numeric[3].includes(".") ? "float" : "int");
      }
      for (const quoted of source.matchAll(/([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*)\s*(==|!=)\s*(['"])(.*?)\3/g)) {
        upsert(quoted[1], "text");
      }
      return [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    function valueTypeLabel(value) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return "array";
      if (value === null) return "null";
      return typeof value;
    }

    function conditionScopeStates(t) {
      const states = [];
      const seen = new Set();
      const add = state => {
        if (!state || seen.has(state.id)) return;
        seen.add(state.id);
        states.push(state);
      };
      const sourceState = byId(effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(t)) || byId(t.from);
      add(sourceState);
      for (let parent = byId(sourceState?.parentId); parent; parent = byId(parent.parentId)) add(parent);
      add(byId(t.from));
      add(byId(effectiveTransitionTargetStateId(t)) || byId(t.to));
      return states;
    }

    function widgetPresetInfo(template) {
      const copy = snapshotStateTemplates([template])[0];
      if (!copy) return null;
      const components = normalizeComponents(copy.components).filter(component => component.type === "daisy");
      if (components.length !== 1) return null;
      const component = components[0];
      const sourceId = String(copy.rootStateId || copy.id || component.variant || copy.title || "widget");
      const sourcePath = stateDataScopeForId(sourceId);
      const sourceLocalPath = component.dataPath === sourcePath
        ? ""
        : stateVariableLocalPath({ id: sourceId }, component.dataPath);
      const key = normalizeId(sourceId || component.variant || copy.title || "widget");
      if (!sourcePath || !key) return null;
      return {
        template: copy,
        component,
        sourcePath,
        sourceLocalPath,
        key,
        label: String(copy.title || component.dataLabel || component.variant || "Baustein")
      };
    }

    function widgetDataPathForState(state, info) {
      const scope = stateDataScopeForId(state?.id || "");
      return scope && info?.key ? `${scope}.${info.key}` : "";
    }

    function widgetComponentMatches(component, info, state = null) {
      if (!component || !info || component.type !== "daisy") return false;
      if (String(component.variant || "") !== String(info.component.variant || "")) return false;
      if (String(component.dataLabel || "") !== info.label) return false;
      if (!state) return true;
      const expectedPath = widgetDataPathForState(state, info);
      return Boolean(expectedPath && normalizeBindingPath(component.dataPath, "") === expectedPath);
    }

    function widgetPresetComponentInState(state, template) {
      const info = widgetPresetInfo(template);
      if (!state || !info) return null;
      return normalizeComponents(state.components).find(component => widgetComponentMatches(component, info, state)) || null;
    }

    function deleteDataObjectPathWithChildren(data, path) {
      const key = normalizeBindingPath(path, "");
      if (!key) return normalizeDataObject(data);
      const source = deleteDataObjectPath(data, key);
      for (const existing of Object.keys(source)) {
        if (existing === key || existing.startsWith(key + ".")) delete source[existing];
      }

    function canvasTextSelectionActive(selection = window.getSelection?.()) {

    function clearCanvasTextSelection() {
      const selection = window.getSelection?.();
      if (selection && selection.rangeCount) selection.removeAllRanges();
    }

    function clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive() {
      const selection = window.getSelection?.();
      if (canvasTextSelectionActive(selection)) selection.removeAllRanges();
    }

    function suppressCanvasTextSelection(durationMs = 900) {
      suppressCanvasTextSelectionUntil = Math.max(suppressCanvasTextSelectionUntil, Date.now() + durationMs);
      clearCanvasTextSelection();
      requestAnimationFrame(clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive);
      setTimeout(clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive, 0);
      setTimeout(clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive, 90);
      setTimeout(clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive, 260);
      setTimeout(clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive, 620);
    }

    document.addEventListener("selectionchange", () => {
      if (Date.now() > suppressCanvasTextSelectionUntil) return;
      clearCanvasTextSelectionIfActive();
    }, { capture: true });

    mapEl.addEventListener("selectstart", evt => {

    function runtimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const url = new URL(APP_URL);
      const hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId });
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room)) hash.set("room", room);
      url.hash = hash.toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function createRuntimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([GENERATED_APP_HTML], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      const hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId });
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room)) hash.set("room", room);
      url.hash = hash.toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function createDetachedRuntimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([GENERATED_APP_HTML], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      url.hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId }).toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function standaloneAppHtml() {
      return buildStandaloneAppHtml(GENERATED_APP_HTML, definitionPayload());
    }

    function standaloneRoomFragment() {
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room) ? new URLSearchParams({ room }).toString() : "";
    }

    function createStandaloneAppUrlFromHtml(html) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([html], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      const roomFragment = standaloneRoomFragment();
      if (roomFragment) url.hash = roomFragment;
      return url.href;
    }


    function writeStandaloneAppToPopup(popup, html) {
      if (!popup || !popup.document) return false;
      try {
        const roomFragment = standaloneRoomFragment();
        if (roomFragment) popup.location.hash = roomFragment;
        popup.document.open();
        popup.document.write(html);
        popup.document.close();
        try { popup.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
        try { popup.focus(); } catch (_) {}
        return true;
      } catch (_) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    function navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html) {
      const url = createStandaloneAppUrlFromHtml(html);
      if (popup) {
        try { popup.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
        try {
          if (popup.location?.replace) popup.location.replace(url);
          else popup.location.href = url;
          try { popup.focus(); } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      try {
        const opened = window.open(url, "_blank");
        if (opened) {
          try { opened.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
      revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url);
      return false;
    }

    function openStandaloneAppWindow() {
      let popup = null;
      try { popup = window.open("about:blank", "_blank"); } catch (_) {}
      let html = "";
      try {
        html = standaloneAppHtml();
      } catch (err) {
        try { popup?.close?.(); } catch (_) {}
        modalAlert(err?.message || "Die App konnte nicht in einem neuen Fenster vorbereitet werden.", "Öffnen fehlgeschlagen");
        return false;
      }
      if (writeStandaloneAppToPopup(popup, html)) return true;
      if (navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html)) return true;
      modalAlert("Der Browser hat das neue Fenster blockiert. Bitte Pop-ups für diese Seite erlauben und erneut tippen.", "Öffnen blockiert");
      return false;
    }

    function revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url) {
      const blobUrl = String(url || "").split("#", 1)[0];
      if (blobUrl && blobUrl !== APP_URL) URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    }

      if (!targetWindow || !sessionId || !payload || typeof payload !== "object") return false;
      targetWindow.postMessage(runtimePayloadForSession(payload, sessionId), RUNTIME_MESSAGE_ORIGIN);
      return true;
    }

    function modelDefinitionValue(value) {

    function normalizeSelection(value = selected) {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
      if ("nodes" in value || "edges" in value) return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges, { allCurrentLayer: value.allCurrentLayer === true });
      if (value.type === "node") return selectionFromParts([value.id], []);
      if (value.type === "edge") return selectionFromParts([], [value.id]);
      if (value.type === "nodes") return selectionFromParts(value.ids, []);
      if (value.type === "items") return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges);
      return null;
    }

    function parseEditorSnapshot(snapshot) {
      const data = JSON.parse(snapshot);
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) throw new Error("Invalid editor history snapshot.");
      return {
        model: data.model,
        selected: data.selected || null,
        currentLayerId: data.currentLayerId || null
      };
    }

    function persistModel(sync = true) {
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return false;

      } catch (_) {}
      if (sync) scheduleSync(false);
      return true;
    }

    function cancelInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
        pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = null;
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = null;
    }

    function flushInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (!pendingInspectorTextCommit) return true;
      const pending = pendingInspectorTextCommit;
      cancelInspectorTextCommit();
      flushingInspectorTextCommit = true;
      let saved = false;
      try {
        saved = saveEditorState(pending.historyKey, pending.sync);
      } finally {
        flushingInspectorTextCommit = false;
      }
      if (saved && pending.draw) scheduleDraw();
      return saved;
    }

    function undoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!undoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();
      const previous = undoStack.pop();
      redoStack.push(current);
      beginHistoryAction();
      restoreHistorySnapshot(previous);
    }

    }

    function isPlainObject(value) {

    function assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error(`${path} must be a string.`);
      if (!allowEmpty && !value.trim()) throw new Error(`${path} must not be empty.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertFiniteNumber(value, path) {
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertNoNullish(value, path = "definition") {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) throw new Error(`${path} must not be null or undefined.`);
      if (typeof value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      if (!["boolean", "number", "object", "string"].includes(typeof value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a JSON value.`);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, index)) throw new Error(`${path}[${index}] must not be an array hole.`);
          assertNoNullish(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (typeof value !== "object") return;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a plain JSON object.`);
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        assertNoNullish(item, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateNullableString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
      return assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

    function transitionTriggerContractKey(transition) {
      const baseKey = transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);
      if (!baseKey) return "";
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
      if (!["change", "event", "realtime", "api"].includes(triggerType)) return baseKey;
      const matchKey = canonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition?.triggerMatch);
      return matchKey ? `${baseKey}|match:${matchKey}` : "";
    }

    function validateTransitionTriggerContracts(transitions) {
      const byState = new Map();
      for (const transition of transitions) {
        const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
        if (triggerType === "flow") continue;
        const stateId = effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(transition);
        if (!byState.has(stateId)) byState.set(stateId, []);
        byState.get(stateId).push(transition);
      }
      for (const [stateId, outgoing] of byState) {
        if (outgoing.some(transition => normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) === "auto") && outgoing.length !== 1) {
          throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions from ${stateId} must contain only one auto transition and no other trigger.`);
        }
        const claims = new Map();
        const eventClaims = new Map();
        for (const transition of outgoing) {
          validateTransitionTriggerMatchContract(transition, `definition.model.transitions.${transition.id}.triggerMatch`);
          const key = transitionTriggerContractKey(transition);
          if (!key) throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} must define one concrete trigger.`);
          const baseKey = transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);
          const matchKey = canonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition.triggerMatch);
          if (claims.has(key)) {
            throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} duplicates trigger ${key} from ${stateId}.`);
          }
          claims.set(key, transition.id);
          if (baseKey && ["change", "event", "realtime", "api"].includes(normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition))) {
            const existing = eventClaims.get(baseKey) || [];
            const hasCatchAll = existing.some(item => item.matchKey === "*");
            if ((matchKey === "*" && existing.length) || (matchKey !== "*" && hasCatchAll)) {
              throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} conflicts with catch-all trigger ${baseKey} from ${stateId}.`);
            }
            const overlapping = matchKey !== "*" ? existing.find(item => transitionTriggerMatchesCanOverlap(item.transition, transition)) : null;
            if (overlapping) {
              throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} overlaps trigger match ${baseKey} from ${stateId}.`);
            }
            existing.push({ id: transition.id, matchKey, transition });
            eventClaims.set(baseKey, existing);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    function validateComponentDataPath(value, path, { allowEmpty = false, stateId = "" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const text = assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty }).trim();
      if (!text) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const clean = validateBindingPath(text, path, { allowEmpty });
      if (!clean) return "";
      const ownerScope = stateId ? stateDataScopeForId(stateId) : "";
      if (ownerScope && clean !== ownerScope && !clean.startsWith(ownerScope + ".")) {
        throw new Error(`${path} must bind data owned by states.${stateId}.`);
      }
      if (!ownerScope && !/^states\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.|$)/.test(clean)) {
        throw new Error(`${path} must be a fully qualified states.<id> data scope.`);
      }
      return clean;
    }

    function validateDataTypePath(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const text = assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty }).trim();
      if (!text) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      return validateBindingPath(text, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

    function validateDataWires(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an array.`);
      return value.slice(0, 80).map((wire, index) => {
        const itemPath = `${path}[${index}]`;
        assertPlainObject(wire, itemPath);
        const sourcePath = validateReadableBusPath(wire.sourcePath || wire.path, `${itemPath}.sourcePath`);
        const role = normalizeDataWireRole(wire.role, "field");
        return {
          id: assertString(wire.id, `${itemPath}.id`),
          sourcePath,
          scopePath: "scopePath" in wire ? validateReadableBusPath(wire.scopePath, `${itemPath}.scopePath`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
          itemPath: "itemPath" in wire ? validateBindingPath(wire.itemPath, `${itemPath}.itemPath`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
          role,
          componentType: normalizeDataWireComponentType(wire.componentType || wire.type, role),
          label: "label" in wire ? assertString(wire.label, `${itemPath}.label`, { allowEmpty: true }) : dataPathLabel(sourcePath)
        };
      });
    }

    function validateContextIdentifier(value, path, { fallback = "item" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return fallback;
      const text = assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty: true }).trim();
      if (!text) return fallback;
      if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(text)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a context name like ite

===== state.html :: definition @ 626702 =====
ath.startsWith(scope + ".");
      });
      if (!owner) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.dataPath must reference declared state data.`);
      const scope = stateDataScopeForId(owner.id);
      const localPath = dataPath === scope ? "" : dataPath.slice(scope.length + 1);
      const value = localPath ? dataObjectValueAtPath(owner.data, localPath) : owner.data;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.dataPath must reference an object.`);
      return { value, path: `${componentPath}.data` };
    }

    function validateTransitionActionBindings(states, transitions) {
      const transitionById = new Map(transitions.map(transition => [transition.id, transition]));
      const claim = (state, transitionId, path) => {
        const transition = transitionById.get(transitionId);
        if (!transition) throw new Error(`${path} must reference an existing transition.`);
        const direct = transition.from === state.id;
        const parentExit = Boolean(
          state.parentId &&
          transition.from === state.parentId &&
          transition.groupExitId === state.id
        );
        if (!direct && !parentExit) throw new Error(`${path} must reference an outgoing transition of ${state.id}.`);
      };

      states.forEach((state, stateIndex) => {
        state.components.forEach((component, componentIndex) => {
          const componentPath = `definition.model.states[${stateIndex}].components[${componentIndex}]`;
          if (component.type === "transitionButton") {
            const transitionId = String(component.transitionId || "").trim();
            if (!transitionId) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.transitionId must not be empty.`);
            claim(state, transitionId, `${componentPath}.transitionId`);
            return;
          }
          if (component.type !== "daisy") return;
          const scopedData = stateDataAtComponentPath(states, state, component, componentPath);
          if (!scopedData) return;
          collectTransitionBindingEntries(scopedData.value, scopedData.path)
            .forEach(entry => claim(state, entry.transitionId, entry.path));
        });
      });
    }

    function validateBoundaryDefinition(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return normalizeBoundaryConfig(null);
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      const allowed = new Set(["entryId", "exitId", "entryDisabled", "exitDisabled", "title", "note"]);
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        if (!allowed.has(key)) throw new Error(`${path}.${key} is not part of the canonical boundary contract.`);
      }
      for (const key of ["entryDisabled", "exitDisabled"]) {
        if (key in value && typeof value[key] !== "boolean") throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must be a boolean.`);
      }
      return {
        entryId: "entryId" in value ? assertString(value.entryId, `${path}.entryId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        exitId: "exitId" in value ? assertString(value.exitId, `${path}.exitId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        entryDisabled: Boolean(value.entryDisabled),
        exitDisabled: Boolean(value.exitDisabled),
        title: "title" in value ? assertString(value.title, `${path}.title`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        note: "note" in value ? assertString(value.note, `${path}.note`, { allowEmpty: true }) : ""
      };
    }

    function validateBlueprintDefinition(data) {
      if (data === null || data === undefined) throw new Error("definition must not be null or undefined.");
      assertPlainObject(data, "definition");
      if (data.kind !== DEFINITION_KIND) throw new Error(`definition.kind must be "${DEFINITION_KIND}".`);
      if (data.schemaVersion !== DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error("definition.schemaVersion must be 2.");
      assertString(data.app, "definition.app");
      assertString(data.savedAt, "definition.savedAt");

      const validated = {
        kind: DEFINITION_KIND,
        schemaVersion: DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        app: data.app,
        savedAt: data.savedAt,
        model: validateModelDefinition(data.model),
        stateTemplates: validateStateTemplatesDefinition(data.stateTemplates),
        camera: { x: 32, y: 32, scale: 1 },
        previewCollapsed: false
      };

      if ("camera" in data) {
        assertPlainObject(data.camera, "definition.camera");
        validated.camera = {
          x: assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.x, "definition.camera.x"),
          y: assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.y, "definition.camera.y"),
          scale: clamp(assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.scale, "definition.camera.scale"), 0.25, 2.4)
        };
      }
      if ("previewCollapsed" in data) {
        if (typeof data.previewCollapsed !== "boolean") throw new Error("definition.previewCollapsed must be a boolean.");
        validated.previewCollapsed = data.previewCollapsed;
      }
      return validated;
    }

    function jsLiteral(value) {
      return JSON.stringify(value)
        .replace(/</g, "\\u003c")
        .replace(/>/g, "\\u003e")
        .replace(/&/g, "\\u0026")
        .replace(/\u2028/g, "\\u2028")
        .replace(/\u2029/g, "\\u2029");
    }

    function saveDefinitionFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      const definition = definitionPayload();
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      const text = JSON.stringify(definition, null, 2) + "\n";
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.state.json`, "application/json");
    }

    async function exportHtmlFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      let text;
      try {
        const payload = await inlineExportImages(definitionPayload());
        const appHtml = typeof GENERATED_APP_HTML === "string" ? GENERATED_APP_HTML : await (await fetch(APP_URL, { cache: "no-store" })).text();
        text = buildStandaloneAppHtml(appHtml, payload);
      } catch (err) {
        await modalAlert(err?.message || "Die eigenständige App konnte nicht exportiert werden.", "Export fehlgeschlagen");
        return;
      }
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.html`, "text/html");
    }

    function parseJsonImportText(text) {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(text);
      } catch (_) {
        throw new Error("Import expects a formal .state.json or .state-component.json file.");
      }
    }

    function validateComponentExportDefinition(data) {
      assertPlainObject(data, "component import");
      if (data.kind !== COMPONENT_EXPORT_KIND) throw new Error(`component import kind must be "${COMPONENT_EXPORT_KIND}".`);
      if (data.schemaVersion !== COMPONENT_EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error("component import schemaVersion must be 1.");
      assertPlainObject(data.component, "component import.component");
      const type = assertString(data.component.type, "component import.component.type");
      if (type === "state") {
        assertPlainObject(data.component.state, "component import.component.state");
        assertString(data.component.state.id, "component import.component.state.id");
        assertString(data.component.state.title, "component import.component.state.title");
        return {
          type,
          state: stateExportSnapshot({
            ...data.component.state,
            parentId: null
          })
        };
      }
      if (type === "preset") {
        throw new Error("Preset components are contract-managed and cannot be imported locally.");
      }
      throw new Error("component import.component.type must be state or preset.");
    }

    function templateFromStateComponentSnapshot(state) {
      const snapshot = stateExportSnapshot({ ...state, parentId: null });
      return snapshotStateTemplates([{
        id: uid("tpl"),
        rootStateId: snapshot.id,
        title: snapshot.title,
        components: snapshot.components,
        data: snapshot.data,
        dataSource: snapshot.dataSource,
        repeat: snapshot.repeat,
        dataWires: snapshot.dataWires,
        subscriptions: snapshot.subscriptions,
        boundary: snapshot.boundary,
        states: [],
        transitions: []
      }])[0];
    }

    function normalizeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids) {
      return [...new Set((Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids])
        .map(id => String(id || "").trim())
        .filter(id => id && byId(id)))];
    }

    function queueRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids) {
      normalizeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids).forEach(id => pendingRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds.add(id));
    }

    function takeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids = []) {
      const result = new Set(normalizeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids));
      pendingRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds.forEach(id => {
        if (byId(id)) result.add(id);
      });
      pendingRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds.clear();
      return [...result];
    }

    function scheduleSync(reset, opts = {}) {
      if (opts.defaultOverrideStateIds) queueRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(opts.defaultOverrideStateIds);
      clearTimeout(syncTimer);
      if (reset) return syncToApp(true, opts);
      syncTimer = setTimeout(() => syncToApp(false), 40);
    }

    function normalizeId(text) {
      return String(text || "state")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]+/g, "_")
        .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "") || "state";
    }

    function isReservedRuntimeId(id) {
      return /^__runtime(?:_|:)/.test(String(id || ""));
    }

    function normalizeSubscriptionPath(value) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (text === "*") return "*";
      return normalizeContextPath(text, "");
    }

    function normalizeSubscriptions(value) {
      const source = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || "").split(/[\n,]+/);
      const seen = new Set();
      const result = [];
      source.forEach(item => {
        const path = normalizeSubscriptionPath(item);
        if (!path || seen.has(path)) return;
        seen.add(path);
        result.push(path);
      });
      return result.slice(0, 64);
    }

    function normalizeSubscriptions(value) {
      const source = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || "").split(/[\n,]+/);
      const seen = new Set();
      const result = [];
      source.forEach(item => {
        const path = normalizeSubscriptionPath(item);
        if (!path || seen.has(path)) return;
        seen.add(path);
        result.push(path);

    }

    function normalizeListItems(items, fallbackText) {
      const allowed = new Set(["text", "link", "image", "note", "divider"]);
      if (Array.isArray(items)) {
        return items
          .filter(item => item && typeof item === "object" && allowed.has(item.type || "text"))
          .map(item => ({
            id: item.id || uid("c"),
            type: item.type || "text",
            text: String(item.text || ""),
            url: String(item.url || "")
          }));
      }
      const lines = String(fallbackText || "").split(/\r?\n/).map(line => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      return lines.map(line => ({
        id: uid("c"),
        type: "text",
        text: line,
        url: ""
      }));
    }


    function isDescendant(parentId, childId) {
      let current = byId(childId);
      while (current) {
        if (current.parentId === parentId) return true;
        current = current.parentId ? byId(current.parentId) : null;
      }
      return false;
    }

    function handleBeforeInput(evt) {
      if (evt.inputType !== "historyUndo" && evt.inputType !== "historyRedo") return;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (evt.inputType === "historyUndo") undoModel();
      else redoModel();
    }

    function requestedDemoEntryId() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        const requested = String(url.searchParams.get("demo") || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (["zustand", "website", "demo", "true", "1"].includes(requested)) return "zustand";
        return "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    function consumeDemoEntryUrl() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        if (!url.searchParams.has("demo")) return;
        url.searchParams.delete("demo");
        const query = url.searchParams.toString();
        window.history.replaceState(null, "", url.pathname + (query ? "?" + query : "") + url.hash);
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    let editorAgentElement = null;
    let editorAgentLoadPromise = null;
    let editorAgentSelectionSnapshot = null;

    function editorAgentServiceUrl(path) {
      const route = String(path || "");
      if (location.hostname === "digitalisierungsplanung.de" || location.hostname === "www.digitalisierungsplanung.de") {
        return "https://realtime.digitalisierungsplanung.de" + route;
      }
      return route;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptUrl() {
      return new URL(editorAgentServiceUrl("/assets/agent-widget.js"), location.href).href;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptContentTypeOk(contentType) {
      const type = String(contentType || "").toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim();
      return !type || [
        "application/javascript",
        "text/javascript",
        "application/ecmascript",
        "text/ecmascript"
      ].includes(type);
    }

    function editorAgentScriptRegisteredError(scriptUrl) {
      return new Error([
        "KI-Assistent konnte nicht geladen werden.",
        `Script: ${scriptUrl}`,
        "Status: Script geladen, aber dp-agent-widget wurde nicht registriert.",
        "Nächster Schritt: Browser-Konsole auf JavaScript-Fehler prüfen."
      ].join("\n"));
    }

    async function editorAgentLoadError(scriptUrl, originalError = null) {
      const lines = [
        "KI-Assistent konnte nicht geladen werden.",
        `Script: ${scriptUrl}`
      ];
      const controller = typeof AbortController === "function" ? new AbortController() : null;
      const timeout = controller ? setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 4500) : null;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(scriptUrl, {
          method: "GET",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",
          signal: controller?.signal
        });
        const statusText = [response.status, response.statusText].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
        lines.push(`Status: HTTP ${statusText}`);
        const contentType = response.header

    function jsLiteral(value) {
      return JSON.stringify(value)
        .replace(/</g, "\\u003c")
        .replace(/>/g, "\\u003e")
        .replace(/&/g, "\\u0026")
        .replace(/\u2028/g, "\\u2028")
        .replace(/\u2029/g, "\\u2029");
    }

    function exportImageCanInline(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text || /^data:image\//i.test(text)) return false;
      if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return false;
      return Boolean(force || looksLikeImagePath(path) || looksLikeImageValue(text));
    }

    async function inlineExportImageUrl(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!exportImageCanInline(text, path, force)) return value;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(IMAGE_INLINE_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",
          headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ url: text })
        });
        if (!response.ok) return value;
        const payload = await response.json();
        const dataUri = String(payload?.dataUri || "");
        return /^data:image\/[a-z0-9.+-]+;base64,/i.test(dataUri) ? dataUri : value;
      } catch (_) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    async function inlineExportImageValue(value, path = "") {
      if (typeof value === "string") return inlineExportImageUrl(value, path);

    function composerTemplateFrom(template) {
      const copy = simpleComponentComposerTemplate(template);
      if (!copy) return null;
      copy.title = String(copy.title || template.title || "Zustandsvorlage");
      copy.description = String(template.description || copy.description || "");
      copy.builtIn = Boolean(template.builtIn);
      return copy;
    }

    function composerTemplateScope(template) {
      return stateDataScopeForId(template?.rootStateId || template?.id || "");
    }

    function composerActualPath(template, value) {
      const raw = normalizeDataTypePath(value, "");
      if (!raw) return "";
      const scope = composerTemplateScope(template);
      if (scope && raw.startsWith(scope + ".")) return raw.slice(scope.length + 1);
      if (raw === scope || raw.startsWith("states.")) return "";
      return raw;
    }

    function composerDisplayPath(template, path) {
      return composerActualPath(template, path) || String(path || "");
    }

    function composerHiddenVariablePath(path) {
      const last = String(path || "").split(".").pop() || "";
      return ["clickedAt", "startedAt", "endsAt", "status", "loading", "done", "ok", "error", "statusCode", "count"].includes(last);
    }

    function scalarListValue(value) {
      return Array.isArray(value) && value.every(item => isScalarDataValue(item));
    }

    function normalizeChoiceItems(value) {
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
      return value.map((item, index) => {
        const source = isPlainObject(item) ? { ...item } : {};
        source.label = String(source.label ?? source.text ?? source.title ?? item ?? `Option ${index + 1}`);
        source.checked = Boolean(source.checked);
        delete source.text;
        delete source.title;
        return source;
      });
    }

    async function inlineExportImages(payload) {
      const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload));
      for (const state of next.model.states || []) {
        const statePath = `states.${state.id}`;
        state.data = await inlineExportImageValue(state.data, statePath);
        for (const component of state.components || []) {
          component.url = await inlineExportImageUrl(
            component.url,
            `${statePath}.components.${component.id || "component"}.url`,
            component.type === "image"
          );
        }
      }
      for (const transition of next.model.transitions || []) {
        transition.set = await inlineExportImageValue(transition.set, "transition.set");
      }
      return next;
    }

    function downloadTextFile(text, filename, type) {
      const blob = new Blob([text], { type });
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = filename;

    function stateExportSnapshot(s) {
      const snapshot = {
        id: String(s.id),
        title: String(s.title || s.id),
        components: normalizeComponents(s.components || []),
        data: normalizeStateDataObject(s.data),
        dataTypes: normalizeDataTypes(s.dataTypes, s.data),
        dataSource: normalizeDataSource(s.dataSource),
        repeat: normalizeRepeatConfig(s.repeat),
        dataWires: normalizeDataWires(s.dataWires),
        subscriptions: normalizeSubscriptions(s.subscriptions),
        boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(s.boundary),
        x: Number(s.x || 0),
        y: Number(s.y || 0)
      };
      if (s.parentId) snapshot.parentId = String(s.parentId);
      return snapshot;
    }

    function transitionExportSnapshot(t) {
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(t);
      const snapshot = {
        id: String(t.id || ""),
        from: String(t.from || ""),
        to: String(t.to || ""),
        label: String(transitionDisplayLabel(t)),
        condition: String(t.condition || ""),
        triggerType,
        triggerEvent: normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent({ ...t, triggerType }),
        timerMs: normalizeTransitionTimerMs(t.timerMs),
        set: normalizeDataObject(t.set)
      };
      if (t.groupEntryId) snapshot.groupEntryId = String(t.groupEntryId);
      if (t.groupExitId) snapshot.groupExitId = String(t.groupExitId);
      const triggerMatch = normalizeTransitionTriggerMatch(t.triggerMatch);
      if (!transitionTriggerMatchIsEmpty(triggerMatch)) snapshot.triggerMatch = triggerMatch;
      return snapshot;
    }

    function isBoundaryFlowTransition(t) {
      return Boolean(t?.boundaryFlow) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.from) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.to);
    }

    function applyTriggerTypeAvailability(select, transition) {
      if (!select || !transition) return;
      for (const option of select.options) {
        const mode = String(option.value || "button");
        const triggerType = mode;
        const triggerEvent = transition.triggerEvent;
        const candidate = {
          ...transition,
          triggerType,
          triggerEvent: ["button", "timer", "auto"].includes(triggerType)
            ? defaultTransitionEvent({ ...transition, triggerType })
            : triggerEvent
        };
        option.disabled = mode !== normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) && modelTransitionTriggerConflict(candidate);
      }
    }

      return "Weiter";

      const label = String(transition?.label || "").trim();
      return label || defaultTransitionLabel();

      return explicit || defaultTransitionLabel();

    function normalizeModel(m) {
      m.version = 2;
      m.name = m.name || "Unbenannter Ablauf";
      m.boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(m.boundary);
      delete m.realtime;
      m.states = Array.isArray(m.states) ? m.states : [];

        used.add(id);
        s.id = id;
        s.title = s.title || s.id;
        delete s.renderMode;
        s.components = normalizeStateComponentDataPaths(s, s.components);
        delete s.body;

        s.components = normalizeStateComponentDataPaths(s, s.components)
          .filter(component => component.type !== "dataWire" || wireIds.has(component.wireId));
        s.subscriptions = normalizeSubscriptions(s.subscriptions);
        s.boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(s.boundary);
        s.x = snapClampToGrid(Number.isFinite(s.x) ? s.x : 100, WORLD_MIN_X, WORLD_MAX_X - nodeWidth(s));
        s.y = snapClampToGrid(Number.isFinite(s.y) ? s.y : 100, WORLD_MIN_Y, WORLD_MAX_Y - NODE_H);
      }
      const ids = new Set(m.states.map(s => s.id));

      for (const s of m.states) {

      }
      const rootEntry = m.boundary.entryId ? stateById.get(m.boundary.entryId) : null;
      const rootExit = m.boundary.exitId ? stateById.get(m.boundary.exitId) : null;
      if (m.boundary.entryId && (!rootEntry || stateParentId(rootEntry) !== null)) m.boundary.entryId = "";
      if (m.boundary.exitId && (!rootExit || stateParentId(rootExit) !== null)) m.boundary.exitId = "";
      for (const s of m.states) {

        if (s.boundary.exitId && (!exit || stateParentId(exit) !== s.id)) s.boundary.exitId = "";
      }

    function childBoundaryState(parent, side) {
      const children = statesInLayer(parent.id);
      const boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(parent.boundary);
      const configuredId = side === "input" ? boundary.entryId : boundary.exitId;
      const configured = children.find(state => state.id === configuredId);
      return configured || null;
    }

    function removeBoundaryFlowsForParent(parentId) {
      const proxyPrefix = `proxy:${parentId}:`;
      model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(transition =>
        transition.boundaryFlow?.parentId !== parentId &&
        !String(transition.from || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix) &&
        !String(transition.to || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix)
      );
    }

    function degroupCompositeState(parent) {
      if (!parent || parent.virtualProxy) return false;
      const children = statesInLayer(parent.id);
      if (!children.length) return false;
      const childIds = new Set(children.map(child => child.id));
      const outerParentId = stateParentId(parent);
      const outerParent = outerParentId ? byId(outerParentId) : rootLayerContainer();
      const outerBoundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(outerParent.rootLayer ? model.boundary : outerParent.boundary);
      const entry = childBoundaryState(parent, "input");
      const exit = childBoundaryState(parent, "output");
      if (!entry || !exit) return false;
      const removedLayerIsOpen = Boolean(currentLayerId && (currentLayerId === parent.id || isDescendant(parent.id, currentLayerId)));

      for (const transition of model.transitions) {
        if (isBoundaryFlowTransition(transition)) continue;
        if (transition.to === parent.id) {
          transition.to = childIds.has(transition.groupEntryId) ? transition.groupEntryId : entry.id;
          transition.groupEntryId = "";
        }
        if (transition.from === parent.id) {
          transition.from = childIds.has(transition.groupExitId) ? transition.groupExitId : exit.id;
          transition.groupExitId = "";
        }
        if (transition.to === outerParentId && transition.groupEntryId === parent.id) transition.groupEntryId = entry.id;
        if (transition.from === outerParentId && transition.groupExitId === parent.id) transition.groupExitId = exit.id;
      }

      for (const child of children) {
        child.parentId = outerParentId || null;
      }
      if (outerBoundary.entryId === parent.id) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "input", entry.id);
      if (outerBoundary.exitId === parent.id) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "output", exit.id);
      if (model.initial === parent.id) model.initial = entry.id;
      removeBoundaryFlowsForParent(parent.id);
      model.states = model.states.filter(state => state.id !== parent.id);
      model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(transition => transition.from !== parent.id && transition.to !== parent.id);

    function applyCamera(options = {}) {
      camera.scale = clampScale(camera.scale);
      const tx = Math.round(camera.x);
      const ty = Math.round(camera.y);
      worldEl.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px, ${ty}px) scale(${camera.scale})`;
      const grid = 24 * camera.scale;
      mapEl.style.backgroundSize = `${grid}px ${grid}px, ${grid}px ${grid}px, 100% 100%, 100% 100%`;
      mapEl.style.backgroundPosition = `${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, ${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, 0 0, 0 0`;
      if (options.persist === false) {
        clearTimeout(cameraSaveTimer);
        cameraSaveTimer = null;
      } else {
        scheduleCameraSave();
      }
    }

    function clampScale(value) {
      return clamp(value, 0.25, 2.4);
    }

    function crispScale(value) {
      return Math.round(clampScale(value) * 100) / 100;
    }

    function wheelDeltaPixels(evt) {
      const lineMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 1 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_LINE;
      const pageMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 2 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_PAGE;
      const modeScale = evt.deltaMode === lineMode ? 16 : evt.deltaMode === pageMode ? 240 : 1;
      const deltaY = Number(evt.deltaY);
      return Number.isFinite(deltaY) ? deltaY * modeScale : 0;
    }

    function wheelZoomFactor(evt) {
      const deltaY = clamp(wheelDeltaPixels(evt), -640, 640);
      return Math.pow(2, -deltaY / WHEEL_ZOOM_PIXEL_DENOMINATOR);
    }

    function zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      const before = screenToWorldXY(clientX, clientY);
      camera.scale = clampScale(nextScale);
      camera.x = clientX - r.left - before.x * camera.scale;
      camera.y = clientY - r.top - before.y * camera.scale;
      applyCamera();
    }

    function zoomAtViewportCenter(nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      zoomAtClientPoint(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, nextScale);
    }

    function zoomByFactor(factor, clientX = null, clientY = null) {
      if (clientX === null || clientY === null) {
        zoomAtViewportCenter(camera.scale * factor);
        return;
      }
      zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, camera.scale * factor);
    }

    function viewportCenterWorld() {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      return {
        x: (r.width / 2 - camera.x) / camera.scale,
        y

    function refreshRuntimeInteractionState() {
      const pending = Boolean(pendingRuntimeModelSyncId);
      frameEl.classList.toggle("runtime-sync-pending", pending);

    }

    function prepareRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || payload.type !== "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return payload;
      const syncId = String(payload.syncId || ++runtimeModelSyncSequence);
      pendingRuntimeModelSyncId = syncId;
      refreshRuntimeInteractionState();
      return payload.syncId === syncId ? payload : { ...payload, syncId };
    }

    function runtimeSyncAtOrAfter(value, expected) {
      const actualNumber = Number(value);
      const expectedNumber = Number(expected);
      if (Number.isSafeInteger(actualNumber) && Number.isSafeInteger(expectedNumber)) {
        return actualNumber >= expectedNumber;
      }
      return String(value || "") === String(expected || "");
    }

    function queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      const type = String(payload.type || "");
      if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      } else if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      }
      pendingFramePayloads.push(payload);
    }

    function pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(payload) {
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return 0;
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") return 1;
      return 2;
    }

    function flushPendingRuntimePayloads() {
      if (!appFrameReady || !frameEl.contentWindow || !appFrameContainsGeneratedApp()) return false;
      const queue = pendingFramePayloads
        .map((payload, index) => ({ payload, index }))
        .sort((a, b) => pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(a.payload) - pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(b.payload) || a.index - b.index)
        .map(item => item.payload);
      pendingFramePayloads = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index += 1) {
        try {
          postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, queue[index], appFrameSessionId);
        } catch (_) {
          pendingFramePayloads.unshift(...queue.slice(index));
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    }

    function handleEditorAgentSnapshot(event) {

    function loadEditorState() {
      try {
        const text = localStorage.getItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!text) return null;
        const data = JSON.parse(text);
        return data && typeof data === "object" && data.model ? data : null;
      } catch (_) { return null; }
    }

    function applyPersistedEditorState(data) {
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(data.model)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      const nextModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.model)));
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(nextModel)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      model = nextModel;
      currentLayerId = data.currentLayerId && byId(data.currentLayerId) ? data.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(data.selected || null);
      return true;
    }

    function loadCamera() {
      try {
        const text = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY + ".camera");
        return text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
      } catch (_) { return null; }
    }

    function cameraSnapshotForPersistence(value = null) {
      const source = value || camera;
      return { x: source.x, y: source.y, scale: source.scale };
    }

    function saveCamera(value = null) {
      try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY + ".camera", JSON.stringify(cameraSnapshotForPersistence(value))); } catch (_) {}
    }

    function scheduleCameraSave() {
      clearTimeout(cameraSaveTimer);
      const snapshot = cameraSnapshotForPersistence();
      cameraSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        cameraSaveTimer = null;
        saveCamera(snapshot);
      }, 140);
    }

    }

    function clampNumber(value, min, max) {
      return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
    }

    function normalizeMobileSheetTopRatio(value) {
      const number = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(number) ? clampNumber(number, 0, 1) : MOBILE_SHEET_DEFAULT_TOP_RATIO;
    }

    function normalizeStoredPanelWidth(value, min, max, fallback) {
      const number = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(number) ? clampNumber(Math.round(number), min, max) : fallback;
    }

    function normalizeStoredPanelHeight(value, min, max, fallback) {
      const number = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(number) ? clampNumber(Math.round(number), min, max) : fallback;
    }

    function mobileSheetTopPxFromRatio() {
      const bounds = mobileSheetBounds();
      const ratio = normalizeMobileSheetTopRatio(mobileSheetTopRatio);
      return clampNumber(Math.round(bounds.height * ratio), 0, bounds.maxTop);
    }

    function setMobileSheetTopPx(topPx, opts = {}) {
      const bounds = mobileSheetBounds();
      const top = clampNumber(Math.round(topPx), 0, bounds.maxTop);
      mobileSheetTopRatio = bounds.height > 0 ? top / bounds.height : MOBILE_SHEET_DEFAULT_TOP_RATIO;
      applyMobileSheetGeometry(opts);
    }

    function snapMobileSheetTopPx(topPx) {
      const bounds = mobileSheetBounds();
      const top = clampNumber(Math.round(topPx), 0, bounds.maxTop);
      if (top <= MOBILE_SHEET_SNAP_PX) return 0;
      if (bounds.maxTop - top <= MOBILE_SHEET_SNAP_PX) return bounds.maxTop;
      return top;
    }

    function applyMobileSheetGeometry(opts = {}) {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout() || mobileWorkspaceView === "canvas") {
        workspaceEl.style.removeProperty("--mobile-sheet-top");
        applyMobilePreviewRuntimeInset();
        return;
      }
      const top = mobileSheetTopPxFromRatio();
      workspaceEl.style.setProperty("--mobile-sheet-top", top + "px");
      if (opts.persist === true) saveUiState();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        updateStateExplorerSearchDragMode();
        applyMobilePreviewRuntimeInset();
      });
    }

    function applyWorkspaceSplitStyle() {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout()) {
        workspaceEl.style.gridTemplateColumns = "";

      }
      workspaceEl.style.gridTemplateColumns = "1fr";
      workspaceEl.style.gridTemplateRows = "minmax(0, 1fr)";

    }

    function updatePanelResize(evt) {
      if (!panelResize || evt.pointerId !== panelResize.pointerId) return false;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const deltaX = evt.clientX - panelResize.startX;
      const deltaY = evt.clientY - panelResize.startY;
      if (panelResize.kind === "mobileSheet") {
        setMobileSheetTopPx(panelResize.startTop + deltaY, { persist: false });
      } else if (panelResize.kind === "inspector") {
        inspectorPanelWidth = panelResize.inspectorWidth + deltaX;
      } else if (panelResize.kind === "stateExplorer") {
        stateExplorerDockHeight = panelResize.stateExplorerHeight - deltaY;
      } else {
        previewPanelWidth = panelResize.previewWidth - deltaX;
      }
      applyPanelWidths({ persist: false });
      return true;
    }

    function finishPanelResize(evt) {
      if (!panelResize || evt.pointerId !== panelResize.pointerId) return false;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
      try { evt.target.releasePointerCapture?.(evt.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
      if (panelResize.kind === "mobileSheet") {
        setMobileSheetTopPx(snapMobileSheetTopPx(mobileSheetTopPxFromRatio()), { persist: true });
        panelResize = null;
        workspaceEl.classList.remove("mobile-sheet-resizing");
        return true;
      }
      panelResize = null;
      workspaceEl.classList.remove("panel-resizing");
      workspaceEl.classList.remove("state-explorer-resizing");
      applyPanelWidths();
      return true;
    }

      if (!panelResize || evt.pointerId !== panelResize.pointerId) return false;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
      try { evt.target.releasePointerCapture?.(evt.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
      if (panelResize.kind === "mobileSheet") {
        setMobileSheetTopPx(snapMobileSheetTopPx(mobileSheetTopPxFromRatio()), { persist: true });
        panelResize = null;
        workspaceEl.classList.remove("mobile-sheet-resizing");
        return true;
      }
      panelResize = null;
      workspaceEl.classList.remove("panel-resizing");
      workspaceEl.classList.remove("state-explorer-resizing");
      applyPanelWidths();
      return true;
    }

    function snapshotTemplateStates(item) {
      return (Array.isArray(item?.states) ? item.states : [])
        .filter(state => state && typeof state === "object")
        .slice(0, 120)
        .map(state => {
          const x = Number(state.x);
          const y = Number(state.y);
          const snapshot = {
            id: String(state.id || uid("state")),
            title: String(state.title || state.id || "Zustand"),
            components: normalizeComponents(state.components || []),
            data: normalizeStateDataObject(state.data),
            dataTypes: normalizeDataTypes(state.dataTypes, state.data),
            dataSource: normalizeDataSource(state.dataSource),
            repeat: normalizeRepeatConfig(state.repeat),
            dataWires: normalizeDataWires(state.dataWires),
            subscriptions: normalizeSubscriptions(state.subscriptions),
            boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(state.boundary),
            x: Number.isFinite(x) ? x : 120,
            y: Number.isFinite(y) ? y : 120
          };
          if (typeof state.parentId === "string" && state.parentId) snapshot.parentId = state.parentId;
          return snapshot;
        });
    }
