===== state.html :: persist @ 583962 =====
function normalizeSelection(value = selected) {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
      if ("nodes" in value || "edges" in value) return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges, { allCurrentLayer: value.allCurrentLayer === true });
      if (value.type === "node") return selectionFromParts([value.id], []);
      if (value.type === "edge") return selectionFromParts([], [value.id]);
      if (value.type === "nodes") return selectionFromParts(value.ids, []);
      if (value.type === "items") return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges);
      return null;
    }

    function editorSnapshotPayload() {
      return {
        model: JSON.parse(modelSnapshot()),
        selected: normalizeSelection(),
        currentLayerId
      };
    }

    function editorSnapshot() {
      return JSON.stringify(editorSnapshotPayload());
    }

    function editorStorageSnapshot() {
      return JSON.stringify({
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        ...editorSnapshotPayload()
      });
    }

    function parseEditorSnapshot(snapshot) {
      const data = JSON.parse(snapshot);
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) throw new Error("Invalid editor history snapshot.");
      return {
        model: data.model,
        selected: data.selected || null,
        currentLayerId: data.currentLayerId || null
      };
    }

    function persistModel(sync = true) {
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return false;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY, editorStorageSnapshot());
      } catch (_) {}
      if (sync) scheduleSync(false);
      return true;
    }

    function cancelInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
        pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = null;
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = null;
    }

    function flushInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (!pendingInspectorTextCommit) return true;
      const pending = pendingInspectorTextCommit;
      cancelInspectorTextCommit();
      flushingInspectorTextCommit = true;
      let saved = false;
      try {
        saved = saveEditorState(pending.historyKey, pending.sync);
      } finally {
        flushingInspectorTextCommit = false;
      }
      if (saved && pending.draw) scheduleDraw();
      return saved;
    }

    function scheduleInspectorTextCommit(historyKey, opts = {}) {
      const next = {
        historyKey,
        sync: opts.sync !== false,
        draw: Boolean(opts.draw)
      };
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit.historyKey !== historyKey) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = pendingInspectorTextCommit
        ? {
            historyKey,
            sync: pendingInspectorTextCommit.sync || next.sync,
            draw: pendingInspectorTextCommit.draw || next.draw
          }
        : next;
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
      pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = setTimeout(flushInspectorTextCommit, opts.delay ?? 120);
    }

    function updateHistoryButtons() {
      const canUndo = Boolean(undoStack.length);
      const canRedo = Boolean(redoStack.length);
      if (btnCanvasUndo) btnCanvasUndo.disabled = !canUndo;
      if (btnCanvasRedo) btnCanvasRedo.disabled = !canRedo;
    }

    function eventComposedElements(evt) {
      const path = typeof evt?.composedPath === "function" ? evt.composedPath() : [];
      const nodes = path.length ? path : [evt?.target];
      return nodes.filter(node => node?.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE);
    }

    function eventPathMatches(evt, selector) {
      return eventComposedElements(evt).some(element => element.matches?.(selector) || element.closest?.(selector));
    }

    function targetElement(target) {
      return target?.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE ? target.parentElement : target;
    }

    function elementIsHistoryTextEditTarget(element) {
      if (!element?.matches) return false;
      if (element.matches("textarea, [contenteditable='true']")) return true;
      if (!element.matches("input")) return false;
      return !new Set(["button", "checkbox", "color", "file", "hidden", "image", "radio", "range", "reset", "submit"]).has(String(element.type || "text").toLowerCase());
    }

    function historyTextEditEvent(evt) {
      return eventComposedElements(evt).some(elementIsHistoryTextEditTarget);
    }

    function navigateToLayer(layerId, historyKey = "layer:navigate", opts = {}) {
      const nextLayerId = layerId && byId(layerId) ? layerId : null;
      if (currentLayerId === nextLayerId) return;
      if (historyKey !== "layer:runtime") {
        stopRuntimeLayerFollow(1200);
      }
      if (historyKey !== "layer:runtime" && historyKey !== "layer:auto-entry") {
        pendingAutoEntryLayerBackPulseId = "";
      }
      currentLayerId = nextLayerId;
      selected = null;
      connecting = null;
      connectingTargetId = null;
      dragging = null;
      pendingEdgeMouse = null;
      stateDragOverStateId = "";
      boxSelecting = null;
      showEmptyInspector();
      updateSelectRect();
      if (opts.persist !== false) saveSelection(historyKey + ":" + (nextLayerId || "root"));
      if (opts.fit === true) {
        if (isMobileWorkspaceLayout()) focusMobileCanvasState({ allowPanelView: true });
        else fitView();
      }
      if (opts.draw === false) return;
      draw();
    }

    function stopRuntimeLayerFollow(duration = 1600) {
      runtimeLayerFollowEnabled = false;
      suppressRuntimeLayerFollowUntil = Date.now() + duration;
      pendingAppStartStateId = null;
      pendingAppStartSuppressLayerFollow = false;
    }

    function enterStateLayer(stateId, opts = {}) {
      if (!["node-dblclick", "inspector"].includes(opts.source)) return;
      if (!opts.force && !opts.explicit && Date.now() < suppressExplicitLayerEnterUntil) return;
      if (!opts.force && !opts.explicit && Date.now() < suppressLayerEnterUntil) return;
      const state = byId(stateId);
      if (!state || state.virtualProxy) return;
      if (!opts.force && stateParentId(state) !== (currentLayerId || null)) return;
      if (!opts.force && (dragging || connecting || panning || boxSelecting)) return;
      suppressLayerExitUntil = Date.now() + 800;
      suppressEmptyCanvasDblClickUntil = Date.now() + 800;
      lastNodeClick = null;
      lastTouchNodeTap = null;
      lastEmptyCanvasClick = null;
      pendingCanvasMouse = null;
      pendingCanvasTouch = null;
      navigateToLayer(stateId, "layer:enter", { fit: true });
    }

    }

    function transitionBetween(from, to, excludeId = null) {
      return model.transitions.find(t => t.from === from && t.to === to && !t.boundaryFlow && !isBoundaryProxyId(t.from) && !isBoundaryProxyId(t.to) && t.id !== excludeId);
    }

    function applyCamera(options = {}) {
      camera.scale = clampScale(camera.scale);
      const tx = Math.round(camera.x);
      const ty = Math.round(camera.y);
      worldEl.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px, ${ty}px) scale(${camera.scale})`;
      const grid = 24 * camera.scale;
      mapEl.style.backgroundSize = `${grid}px ${grid}px, ${grid}px ${grid}px, 100% 100%, 100% 100%`;
      mapEl.style.backgroundPosition = `${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, ${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, 0 0, 0 0`;
      if (options.persist === false) {
        clearTimeout(cameraSaveTimer);
        cameraSaveTimer = null;
      } else {
        scheduleCameraSave();
      }
    }

    function clampScale(value) {
      return clamp(value, 0.25, 2.4);
    }

    function crispScale(value) {
      return Math.round(clampScale(value) * 100) / 100;
    }

    function wheelDeltaPixels(evt) {
      const lineMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 1 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_LINE;
      const pageMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 2 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_PAGE;
      const modeScale = evt.deltaMode === lineMode ? 16 : evt.deltaMode === pageMode ? 240 : 1;
      const deltaY = Number(evt.deltaY);
      return Number.isFinite(deltaY) ? deltaY * modeScale : 0;
    }

    function wheelZoomFactor(evt) {
      const deltaY = clamp(wheelDeltaPixels(evt), -640, 640);
      return Math.pow(2, -deltaY / WHEEL_ZOOM_PIXEL_DENOMINATOR);
    }

    function zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      const before = screenToWorldXY(clientX, clientY);
      camera.scale = clampScale(nextScale);
      camera.x = clientX - r.left - before.x * camera.scale;
      camera.y = clientY - r.top - before.y * camera.scale;
      applyCamera();
    }

    function zoomAtViewportCenter(nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      zoomAtClientPoint(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, nextScale);
    }

    function zoomByFactor(factor, clientX = null, clientY = null) {
      if (clientX === null || clientY === null) {
        zoomAtViewportCenter(camera.scale * factor);
        return;
      }
      zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, camera.scale * factor);
    }

    function viewportCenterWorld() {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      return {
        x: (r.width / 2 - camera.x) / camera.scale,
        y: (r.height / 2 - camera.y) / camera.scale
      };
    }

    function keepViewportCenteredOn(worldPoint) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      camera.x = Math.round(r.width / 2 - worldPoint.x * camera.scale);
      camera.y = Math.round(r.height / 2 - worldPoint.y * camera.scale);
      applyCamera();
    }

    function togglePreviewCollapsed() {
      const center = viewportCenterWorld();
      previewCollapsed = !previewCollapsed;
      applyPreviewCollapsed();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => keepViewportCenteredOn(center));
      setTimeout(() => keepViewportCenteredOn(center), 210);
    }

    function resetView() {
      camera = { x: 32, y: 32, scale: 1 };
      applyCamera();
    }

    function mobileRuntimeFollowRect() {
      const mapRect = mapEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      let height = mapRect.height;
      if (mobileWorkspaceView !== "canvas") {
        const panelSelector = mobileWorkspaceView === "app" ? ".preview" : mobileWorkspaceView === "edit" ? "#stateInspector" : "#stateExplorer";
        const panel = document.querySelector(panelSelector)?.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (panel && panel.top > mapRect.top + 140) {
          height = Math.max(128, Math.min(mapRect.height, panel.top - mapRect.top - 16));
        }
      }
      return {
        width: Math.max(1, mapRect.width),
        height: Math.max(1, height)
      };
    }

    function focusMobileRuntimeFollow(previousStateId, runtimeStateId, transitionId = "") {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout() || !mobileRuntimeFollow || !runtimeStateId) return false;
      const runtimeState = byId(runtimeStateId);
      if (!runtimeState || runtimeState.virtualProxy) return false;
      const runtimeLayerId = stateParentId(runtimeState);
      let changedLayer = false;
      if ((currentLayerId || null) !== runtimeLayerId) {
        navigateToLayer(runtimeLayerId, "layer:runtime-mobile", { fit: false, persist: false, draw: false });
        changedLayer = true;
      }
      const ids = new Set([runtimeStateId]);
      const transition = transitionId ? model.transitions.find(item => item.id === transitionId) : null;
      if (transition) {
        ids.add(transition.from);
        ids.add(transition.to);
      }
      if (previousStateId) ids.add(previousStateId);
      const states = [...ids]
        .map(id => byId(id))
        .filter(state => state && !state.virtualProxy && stateParentId(state) === (currentLayerId || null));
      if (!states.length) return false;
      const rect = mobileRuntimeFollowRect();
      const minX = Math.min(...states.map(state => state.x));
      const minY = Math.min(...states.map(state => state.y));
      const maxX = Math.max(...states.map(state => state.x + nodeWidth(state)));
      const maxY = Math.max(...states.map(state => state.y + nodeHeight(state)));
      const width = Math.max(1, maxX - minX);
      const height = Math.max(1, maxY - minY);
      const fittedScale = Math.min(
        MOBILE_RUNTIME_FOLLOW_MAX_SCALE,
        (rect.width - 64) / Math.max(1, width + 64),
        (rect.height - 48) / Math.max(1, height + 64)
      );
      const targetScale = Math.max(MOBILE_RUNTIME_FOLLOW_MIN_SCALE, fittedScale);
      const currentScale = Number.isFinite(camera.scale) ? camera.scale : targetScale;
      const scale = clampScale(Math.min(currentScale, targetScale));
      camera.scale = scale;
      camera.x = Math.round((rect.width - width * scale) / 2 - minX * scale);
      camera.y = Math.round((rect.height - height * scale) / 2 - minY * scale);
      applyCamera({ persist: false });
      if (changedLayer) draw();
      return true;
    }

    function fitView() {
      const states = visibleStates();
      if (!states.length) return resetView();
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      const minX = Math.min(...states.map(s => s.x));
      const minY = Math.min(...states.map(s => s.y));
      const maxX = Math.max(...states.map(s => s.x + nodeWidth(s)));
      const maxY = Math.max(...states.map(s => s.y + nodeHeight(s)));
      const w = Math.max(1, maxX - minX);
      const h = Math.max(1, maxY - minY);
      const pad = 120;
      const rawScale = Math.min(r.width / (w + pad * 2), r.height / (h + pad * 2));
      const scale = rawScale >= 0.92 ? 1 : crispScale(rawScale);
      camera.scale = scale;
      camera.x = Math.round((r.width - w * scale) / 2 - minX * scale);
      camera.y = Math.round((r.height - h * scale) / 2 - minY * scale);
      applyCamera();
    }

    function routeSegments(points) {
      const compact = points.map(snapPointToGrid);
      const segments = [];
      for (let i = 1; i < compact.length; i++) {
        const a = compact[i - 1];
        const b = compact[i];
        if (a.x === b.x && a.y === b.y) continue;
        const vertical = a.x === b.x;
        const horizontal = a.y === b.y;
        if (!vertical && !horizontal) continue;
        segments.push({
          index: i,
          orientation: vertical ? "vertical" : "horizontal",
          coordinate: vertical ? a.x : a.y,
          min: vertical ? Math.min(a.y, b.y) : Math.min(a.x, b.x),
          max: vertical ? Math.max(a.y, b.y) : Math.max(a.x, b.x),
          laneable: i > 1 && i < compact.length - 1
        });
      }
      return segments;
    }

    function laneSlot(index) {
      if (index <= 0) return 0;
      return index % 2 ? Math.ceil(index / 2) : -index / 2;
    }

    // Per-frame route table — populated fresh at the start of every draw() call.
    // Never persisted across frames. No caching, no snapshots.
    let _frameRoutes = new Map();
    let _frameRouteTransitions = new Map();
    let _frameRouteOrder = new Map();
    let _frameVisibleTransitions = null;
    let _framePortTransitionOrders = new Map();
    let _frameRouteBlockingBoxes = new Map();

    function recordRouteMetric(name, amount = 1) {
      window.__stateBlueprintRouteMetrics = window.__stateBlueprintRouteMetrics || {};
      const metrics = window.__stateBlueprintRouteMetrics;
      if (metrics && typeof metrics === "object") metrics[name] = (metrics[name] || 0) + amount;
    }

    function recordPerfMetric(name, amount = 1) {
      const metrics = window.__stateBlueprintPerfMetrics;
      if (metrics && typeof metrics === "object") metrics[name] = (metrics[name] || 0) + amount;
    }

    function transitionRouteKey(transition) {
      return String(transition?.id || "");
    }

    function frameRouteTransition(routeKey) {
      return _frameRouteTransitions.get(routeKey) || model.transitions.find(item => item.id === routeKey) || null;
    }

    function edgeOrder(routeKey) {
      const transition = frameRouteTransition(routeKey);
      const index = _frameRouteOrder.get(transition?.id);
      return Number.isFinite(index) && index >= 0 ? index : Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
    }

    function routeSegmentRecords(routes) {
      const records = [];
      for (const [transitionId, points] of routes) {
        for (const segment of routeSegments(points)) {
          records.push({ ...segment, transitionId });
        }
      }
      return records;
    }

    function overlappingRouteGroups(routes) {
      const buckets = new Map();
      for (const segment of routeSegmentRecords(routes)) {
        if (!segment.laneable) continue;
        const key = `${segment.orientation}:${segment.coordinate}`;
        if (!buckets.has(key)) buckets.set(key, []);
        buckets.get(key).push(segment);
      }

      const groups = [];
      for (const bucket of buckets.values()) {
        const sorted = bucket
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => a.min - b.min || a.max - b.max || edgeOrder(a.transitionId) - edgeOrder(b.transitionId));
        let group = [];
        let groupMax = -Infinity;
        for (const segment of sorted) {
          if (!group.length || segment.min < groupMax) {
            group.push(segment);
            groupMax = Math.max(groupMax, segment.max);
          } else {
            if (new Set(group.map(item => item.transitionId)).size > 1) groups.push(group);
            group = [segment];
            groupMax = segment.max;
          }
        }
        if (new Set(group.map(item => item.transitionId)).size > 1) groups.push(group);
      }
      return groups;
    }

        routed[segment.index - 1].y = coordinate;
        routed[segment.index].y = coordinate;

    function computeFrameRoutes(options = {}) {
      const metricName = options.metricName || "finalRouteBuilds";
      if (metricName) recordRouteMetric(metricName);
      const routeOptions = { ...options };
      const transitions = Array.isArray(options.transitions) ? options.transitions : visibleTransitions();

      _framePortTransitionOrders = new Map();

        routeOptions.allowDenseGridSearch = false;

      const routes = new Map();

      _frameRouteOrder = new Map(model.transitions.map((transition, index) => [transition.id, index]));
      _frameRouteBlockingBoxes = new Map();
      _frameRouteBlockingBoxesEnabled = true;
      try {
        for (const transition of transitions) {
          const from = byId(transition.from);
          const to = byId(transition.to);
          if (!from || !to) continue;

          _frameRouteTransitions.set(routeKey, transition);

        }
        return resolveRouteOverlaps(routes, routeOptions);
      } finally {
        _frameRouteBlockingBoxesEnabled = false;
      }
    }

    function requestFastRouteDrawWindow(duration = 1600) {
      fastRouteDrawUntil = Math.max(fastRouteDrawUntil, Date.now() + duration);

    function edgeRoutePoints(from, to, transition = null) {
      if (!transition) return baseEdgeRoutePoints(from, to, transition);
      return _frameRoutes.get(transitionRouteKey(transition)) || baseEdgeRoutePoints(from, to, transition);
    }

    function polylinePoint(points, ratio) {
      const compact = points.map(snapPointToGrid).filter((point, index, list) => {
        const previous = list[index - 1];
        return !previous || previous.x !== point.x || previous.y !== point.y;
      });
      const lengths = [];
      let total = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < compact.length; i++) {
        const length = Math.hypot(compact[i].x - compact[i - 1].x, compact[i].y - compact[i - 1].y);
        lengths.push(length);
        total += length;
      }
      if (!total) return compact[0] || { x: 0, y: 0 };
      let remaining = total * clamp(ratio, 0, 1);
      for (let i = 1; i < compact.length; i++) {
        const length = lengths[i - 1];
        if (remaining <= length) {
          const start = compact[i - 1];
          const end = compact[i];
          const t = length ? remaining / length : 0;
          return snapPointToGrid({
            x: start.x + (end.x - start.x) * t,
            y: start.y + (end.y - start.y) * t
          });
        }
        remaining -= length;
      }
      return compact[compact.length - 1];
    }

    function edgeTipHandlePoint(from, to, transition = null) {
      return polylinePoint(edgeRoutePoints(from, to, transition), 0.82);
    }

    function updateLayerFrameComment(parent, frameLeft, frameTop, frameWidth) {
      if (!layerFrameCommentEl) return;

      layerFrameCommentEl.style.top = "0px";
      const height = layerFrameCommentEl.offsetHeight || GRID_SIZE * 2;
      const outsideTop = frameTop - height - Math.round(GRID_SIZE * 0.75);
      const top = outsideTop > WORLD_MIN_Y + GRID_SIZE ? outsideTop : frameTop + GRID_SIZE;
      layerFrameCommentEl.style.top = top + "px";
    }

    function isLayerFrameCommentEditing() {
      const active = document.activeElement;
      return Boolean(active && layerFrameCommentEl?.contains(active));
    }

    function updateLayerFrameComment(parent, frameLeft, frameTop, frameWidth) {
      if (!layerFrameCommentEl) return;
      const annotation = layerFrameAnnotation(parent);
      const editing = isLayerFrameCommentEditing();
      layerFrameCommentTitleEl.hidden = false;
      layerFrameCommentBodyEl.hidden = false;
      if (!editing) {
        layerFrameCommentTitleEl.value = annotation.title;
        layerFrameCommentBodyEl.value = annotation.note;
      }
      syncLayerFrameCommentEmptyState();
      layerFrameCommentEl.hidden = false;
      const maxWidth = Math.max(GRID_SIZE * 10, Math.min(GRID_SIZE * 22, frameWidth - GRID_SIZE * 2));
      const left = frameLeft + GRID_SIZE;
      layerFrameCommentEl.style.maxWidth = maxWidth + "px";
      layerFrameCommentEl.style.left = left + "px";
      layerFrameCommentEl.style.top = "0px";
      const height = layerFrameCommentEl.offsetHeight || GRID_SIZE * 2;
      const outsideTop = frameTop - height - Math.round(GRID_SIZE * 0.75);
      const top = outsideTop > WORLD_MIN_Y + GRID_SIZE ? outsideTop : frameTop + GRID_SIZE;
      layerFrameCommentEl.style.top = top + "px";
    }

    function setupLayerFrameCommentEditing() {

          syncLayerFrameCommentInputEmptyState(el);

            evt.preventDefault();

        });
      });
      syncLayerFrameCommentEmptyState();
    }

    function syncPortVisuals(portOverlay) {
      const visualOverlay = getPortVisualOverlay();
      const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
      const sync = createSvgSync(visualOverlay, fragment, {
        createMetric: "portVisualElementsCreated",
        reuseMetric: "portVisualElementsReused",
        removeMetric: "portVisualElementsRemoved"
      });
      for (const source of portOverlay.querySelectorAll(".svg-port")) {
        const sourceKey = source.getAttribute("data-view-key") || "";
        if (!sourceKey) continue;
        const visual = syncSvgElement(sync, `visual:${sourceKey}`, "g", {
          transform: source.getAttribute("transform"),
          class: [...source.classList].filter(name => name !== "svg-port").concat("port-visual").join(" "),
          style: source.getAttribute("style"),
          "data-state-id": source.getAttribute("data-state-id"),
          "data-port-side": source.getAttribute("data-port-side"),
          "aria-hidden": "true"
        });
        visual.replaceChildren(...[...source.children]
          .filter(child => !child.classList.contains("svg-port-hit"))
          .map(child => child.cloneNode(true)));
      }
      finishSvgSync(sync);
    }

    function showEmptyInspector() {
      inspectorStateId = null;
      inspectorEdgeId = null;
      transitionInspectorKeyboardEditing = false;
      stateInspectorEl.classList.remove("template-inspector", "transition-inspector");
      stateInspectorTitleEl.textContent = "Eigenschaften";
      stateInspectorBodyEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="inspector-empty">
          <strong>Kein Zustand ausgewählt</strong>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    async function fetchRealtimeEvents(options = {}) {
      await fetchProductContract({ force: options.force === true, notify: false });
      const events = productContractRealtimeEvents();
      if (!events.length) throw new Error("Ereigniskatalog ist leer");
      return events;
    }

    function refreshInspectorForSelection() {
      const stateId = singleSelectedNodeId();
      const edgeId = singleSelectedEdgeId();
      const state = stateId ? byId(stateId) : null;
      const edge = edgeId ? edgeById(edgeId) : null;
      if (state) showNodeInspector(state, { focus: false });
      else if (edge) showEdgeInspector(edge, { focus: false });
      else showEmptyInspector();
    }

    function bindDataSourceEditor(owner, root, persistOwner, historyPrefix = "state") {
      const url = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceUrl");
      const target = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceTarget");
      const select = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceSelect");
      const preview = root.querySelector("#pDataSourcePreview");
      const inspect = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceInspect");
      const status = root.querySelector("#pFetchStatus");
      if (!url || !target || !select || !preview) return;
      owner.dataSource = normalizeDataSource(owner.dataSource);
      url.value = owner.dataSource.url;
      target.value = owner.dataSource.target;
      select.value = owner.dataSource.select;
      let inspectTimer = 0;
      let inspectToken = 0;
      const update = (persist = true) => {
        const previous = normalizeDataSource(owner.dataSource);
        const next = normalizeDataSource({
          ...owner.dataSource,
          url: url.value,
          target: target.value,
          select: select.value
        });
        const sourceChanged = dataSourceSignature(previous) !== dataSourceSignature(next);
        owner.dataSource = next;
        if (sourceChanged && typeof refreshInspectorGlobalStateTree === "function") refreshInspectorGlobalStateTree();
        preview.textContent = dataSourcePreviewText(next, target.value, select.value);
        preview.classList.toggle("empty", !String(next.url || "").trim());
        if (status) {
          const hasSource = Boolean(String(next.url || "").trim());
          status.textContent = hasSource ? `API: ${dataPathLabel(next.target, "Daten")}` : "Keine API";
          status.title = hasSource ? `${next.target}.data` : "";
          status.classList.toggle("active", hasSource);
        }
        if (persist) persistOwner(`${historyPrefix}:data-source`);
        scheduleInspect();
      };
      const scheduleInspect = () => {
        if (!inspect || inspect.hidden) return;
        clearTimeout(inspectTimer);
        if (!url.value.trim()) {
          inspect.textContent = "URL eintragen oder Antwort einfügen, um Listen und Felder zu wählen.";
          inspect.classList.add("empty");
          return;
        }
        inspect.textContent = "Vorschau wird geladen...";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        const token = ++inspectToken;
        inspectTimer = setTimeout(() => inspectJsonEndpoint(owner, root, persistOwner, token, () => inspectToken, historyPrefix), 350);
      };
      url.oninput = target.oninput = select.oninput = () => update(true);
      update(false);
    }

    function dataPathLabel(path, fallback = "Wert") {
      const clean = normalizeBindingPath(path, "");
      if (clean === "state.current") return "Aktueller Zustand";
      const parts = String(path || "").split(".").filter(Boolean).filter(part => !/^\d+$/.test(part));
      const raw = parts.pop() || fallback;
      const spaced = raw
        .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2")
        .replace(/[_-]+/g, " ")
        .trim();
      return (spaced || fallback).replace(/^./, ch => ch.toUpperCase());
    }

    function dataPathLabel(path, fallback = "Wert") {

    function friendlyDataSourceLabel(source) {
      const text = String(source || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!text) return "Geteilte Daten";
      if (text === "current" || text === "mapped") return "In der Darstellung";
      if (text === "state data" || text === "state") return "Dieser Zustand";
      if (text === "previous state" || text === "from state" || text === "source state") return "Vorheriger Zustand";
      if (text === "parent context" || text === "parent state") return "Übergeordneter Zustand";
      if (text === "target state") return "Zielzustand";
      if (text === "runtime" || text === "runtime global state" || text === "app flow" || text === "screen flow") return "Ablauf";
      if (text === "repeat" || text === "repeat item") return "Ausgewählte Liste";
      if (text.includes("fetch")) return "API-Daten";
      if (text.includes("realtime")) return "Realtime";
      if (text === "subscribed") return "Beobachteter Pfad";
      if (text === "global" || text === "globalstate" || text === "global state" || text === "shared data") return "Geteilte Daten";
      return source;
    }

    function dataPathOptionLabel(path, fallback = "Feld", source = "") {
      const clean = normalizeBindingPath(path, "");
      const label = dataPathLabel(clean, fallback);
      const from = friendlyDataSourceLabel(source);
      if (clean === "state.current") return label;
      return clean ? `${label} (${clean})${from ? " - " + from : ""}` : label;
    }

          if (state === owner) continue;
          stateDataBusEntries(state).forEach(({ busPath, value }) => collectRepeatArrayCandidates(value, busPath, candidates, 0, state.title || state.id || "state"));

      if (hasDataSource) candidates.push({ path: `${target}.data`, source: "fetch response assumption", score: 35, assumed: true });
      const seen = new Set();
      return candidates
        .filter(item => normalizeContextPath(item.path, ""))
        .filter(item => {
          if (seen.has(item.path)) return false;
          seen.add(item.path);
          return true;
        })
        .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
    }

    function safeEditorFetchUrl(url) {
      const raw = String(url || "").trim();
      if (!raw) return "";
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(raw, window.location.href);
        return /^https?:$/i.test(parsed.protocol) ? parsed.href : "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    async function inspectJsonEndpoint(owner, root, persistOwner, token, latestToken, historyPrefix = "state") {
      const inspect = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceInspect");
      const urlInput = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceUrl");
      if (!inspect || !urlInput || token !== latestToken()) return;
      const trimmed = urlInput.value.trim();
      if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
        try {
          const json = JSON.parse(trimmed);
          if (token !== latestToken()) return;
          renderJsonInspect(owner, root, persistOwner, json, {
            status: "parsed",
            contentType: "application/json"
          }, { historyPrefix });
        } catch (err) {
          if (token !== latestToken()) return;
          inspect.textContent = "Pasted response is not valid data: " + err.message;
          inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        }
        return;
      }
      const requestUrl = safeEditorFetchUrl(urlInput.value);
      if (!requestUrl) {
        inspect.textContent = "Use an http(s) URL or paste a JSON response.";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        return;
      }
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);
      try {
        const response = await fetch(requestUrl, {
          headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "same-origin",
          signal: controller.signal
        });
        const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
        const json = await response.json();
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        renderJsonInspect(owner, root, persistOwner, json, {
          status: response.status,
          contentType
        }, { historyPrefix });
      } catch (err) {
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        inspect.textContent = (err?.name === "AbortError")
          ? "Vorschau hat zu lange gebraucht. Die App versucht es beim Ausführen erneut."
          : "Datenvorschau nicht möglich.

          persistOwner(`${historyPrefix}:repeat`);

    async function inspectJsonEndpoint(owner, root, persistOwner, token, latestToken, historyPrefix = "state") {
      const inspect = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceInspect");
      const urlInput = root.querySelector("#pDataSourceUrl");
      if (!inspect || !urlInput || token !== latestToken()) return;
      const trimmed = urlInput.value.trim();
      if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
        try {
          const json = JSON.parse(trimmed);
          if (token !== latestToken()) return;
          renderJsonInspect(owner, root, persistOwner, json, {
            status: "parsed",
            contentType: "application/json"
          }, { historyPrefix });
        } catch (err) {
          if (token !== latestToken()) return;
          inspect.textContent = "Pasted response is not valid data: " + err.message;
          inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        }
        return;
      }
      const requestUrl = safeEditorFetchUrl(urlInput.value);
      if (!requestUrl) {
        inspect.textContent = "Use an http(s) URL or paste a JSON response.";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        return;
      }
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);
      try {
        const response = await fetch(requestUrl, {
          headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "same-origin",
          signal: controller.signal
        });
        const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
        const json = await response.json();
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        renderJsonInspect(owner, root, persistOwner, json, {
          status: response.status,
          contentType
        }, { historyPrefix });
      } catch (err) {
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        inspect.textContent = (err?.name === "AbortError")
          ? "Vorschau hat zu lange gebraucht. Die App versucht es beim Ausführen erneut."
          : "Datenvorschau nicht möglich. Prüfe CORS, URL oder ob der Endpunkt JSON liefert.";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }

    function jsonValueAtPath(value, path) {
      const parts = String(path || "").split(".").filter(Boolean);
      let current = value;
      for (const part of parts) {
        if (current === null || current === undefined || typeof current !== "object" || !(part in current)) return undefined;
        current = current[part];
      }
      return current;
    }

    function collectArrayPaths(value, prefix = "", out = [], depth = 0) {
      if (out.length > 24 || depth > 5) return out;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        out.push({ path: prefix, count: value.length });
        if (value.length) collectArrayPaths(value[0], prefix, out, depth + 1);
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return out;
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 24)) {
        const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key;
        collectArrayPaths(child, path, out, depth + 1);
      }
      return out;
    }

      if (!requestUrl) {
        inspect.textContent = "Use an http(s) URL or paste a JSON response.";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
        return;
      }
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);
      try {
        const response = await fetch(requestUrl, {
          headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "same-origin",
          signal: controller.signal
        });
        const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
        const json = await response.json();
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        renderJsonInspect(owner, root, persistOwner, json, {
          status: response.status,
          contentType
        }, { historyPrefix });
      } catch (err) {
        if (token !== latestToken()) return;
        inspect.textContent = (err?.name === "AbortError")
          ? "Vorschau hat zu lange gebraucht. Die App versucht es beim Ausführen erneut."
          : "Datenvorschau nicht möglich. Prüfe CORS, URL oder ob der Endpunkt JSON liefert.";
        inspect.classList.remove("empty");
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }

    function jsonValueAtPath(value, path) {
      const parts = String(path || "").split(".").filter(Boolean);
      let current = value;
      for (const part of parts) {
        if (current === null || current === undefined || typeof current !== "object" || !(part in current)) return undefined;
        current = current[part];
      }
      return current;
    }

    function collectArrayPaths(value, prefix = "", out = [], depth = 0) {
      if (out.length > 24 || depth > 5) return out;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        out.push({ path: prefix, count: value.length });
        if (value.length) collectArrayPaths(value[0], prefix, out, depth + 1);
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return out;
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 24)) {
        const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key;
        collectArrayPaths(child, path, out, depth + 1);
      }
      return out;
    }

    function collectLeafPaths(value, prefix = "", out = [], depth = 0) {
      if (out.length > 30 || depth > 5) return out;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        if (value.length) collectLeafPaths(value[0], prefix, out, depth + 1);
        return out;
      }
      if (isPlainObject(value)) {
        for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 24)) {
          const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key;
          collectLeafPaths(child, path, out, depth + 1);
        }
        return out;
      }
      if (prefix) out.push(prefix);
      return out;
    }

    function collectArrayPaths(value, prefix = "", out = [], depth = 0) {
      if (out.length > 24 || depth > 5) return out;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        out.push({ path: prefix, count: value.length });
        if (value.length) collectArrayPaths(value[0], prefix, out, depth + 1);
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return out;

        const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key;
        collectArrayPaths(child, path, out, depth + 1);
      }
      return out;
    }

    function collectLeafPaths(value, prefix = "", out = [], depth = 0) {
      if (out.length > 30 || depth > 5) return out;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        if (value.length) collectLeafPaths(value[0], prefix, out, depth + 1);

      return out;

    function conditionInputSummary(condition) {
      const inputs = inferConditionInputs(condition);
      if (!inputs.length) return "Keine Eingaben. Immer verfügbar.";
      return "Eingaben: " + inputs.map(input => `${input.name}:${input.type}`).join(", ");
    }

    function requestedDemoEntryId() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        const requested = String(url.searchParams.get("demo") || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (["zustand", "website", "demo", "true", "1"].includes(requested)) return "zustand";
        return "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    function consumeDemoEntryUrl() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        if (!url.searchParams.has("demo")) return;
        url.searchParams.delete("demo");
        const query = url.searchParams.toString();
        window.history.replaceState(null, "", url.pathname + (query ? "?" + query : "") + url.hash);
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    function demoLabelForId(demoId) {
      return "Digitalisierungsplanung";
    }

    async function exampleEntryModelForId(entryId) {
      return normalizeModel(exampleWebsiteModel());
    }

    function modelMatchesExampleEntry(entryId) {
      if (entryId !== "zustand") return false;
      return JSON.stringify(modelDefinitionValue(model)) === JSON.stringify(modelDefinitionValue(exampleWebsiteModel()));
    }

    async function loadExampleEntryById(entryId, shouldFit = true) {
      const nextModel = await exampleEntryModelForId(entryId);
      loadEditorModel(nextModel, shouldFit);
    }

    }

    function editorAgentServiceUrl(path) {
      const route = String(path || "");
      if (location.hostname === "digitalisierungsplanung.de" || location.hostname === "www.digitalisierungsplanung.de") {
        return "https://realtime.digitalisierungsplanung.de" + route;
      }
      return route;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptUrl() {
      return new URL(editorAgentServiceUrl("/assets/agent-widget.js"), location.href).href;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptContentTypeOk(contentType) {
      const type = String(contentType || "").toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim();

        "application/javascript",
        "text/javascript",
        "application/ecmascript",
        "text/ecmascript"
      ].includes(type);

    function isTemplateCanvasDrop(evt) {
      if (!hasTemplateDrag(evt)) return false;
      if (!pointInElement(evt.clientX, evt.clientY, mapEl)) return false;
      if (evt.target?.closest?.(".node, .inner-state-dropzone")) return false;
      return true;
    }

    document.addEventListener("dragover", evt => {
      if (!isTemplateCanvasDrop(evt)) return;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "copy";
    }, { capture: true });

    document.addEventListener("drop", evt => {
      if (!isTemplateCanvasDrop(evt)) return;
      const templateId = templateIdFromDrag(evt);
      if (!templateId) return;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopPropagation();
      addTemplateAt(templateId, evt.clientX, evt.clientY);
      clearTemplateDragState();
    }, { capture: true });
